3407	  /** use 期间 bodyFrame 行（Player.cs:35874-35990，itemAnimation>0 分支逐 useStyle）。
3408	   *  -1 = 不改 bodyFrame（useStyle 10/14/15 被 flag6 排除在 :35716、16 无匹配分支），
3409	   *  交给常规动画行（行走/跳跃）。aim 仅 useStyle 5 用（:35986 itemRotation*direction 档） */
3410	  private useStyleBodyRow(swing: { t: number; dur: number; useStyle?: number; aim?: number }, p: Player): number {
3411	    const us = swing.useStyle ?? 1;
3412	    const pAnim = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, swing.t / swing.dur));
3413	    switch (us) {
3414	      case 1:
3415	      case 13:
3416	        // :35882-35890 / :35971-35985：三段 行1/2/3（13 与 1 同表）
3417	        return pAnim > 2 / 3 ? 1 : pAnim > 1 / 3 ? 2 : 3;
3418	      case 2:
3419	      case 3:
3420	        return 3; // :35908 / :35948（3 两段都是行3）
3421	      case 4:
3422	        return 2; // :35959 举过头
3423	      case 9:
3424	        return 0; // :35927 饮用
3425	      case 6: {
3426	        // :35931-35947：(1-pAnim)*6 截 1，≥0.5 → 行3 否则行2
3427	        const n = Math.min((1 - pAnim) * 6, 1);
3428	        return n >= 0.5 ? 3 : 2;
3429	      }
3430	      case 5: {
3431	        // :35986-36005：默认行3；itemRotation*dir < -0.75 → 行2、> 0.6 → 行4
3432	        const n21 = (swing.aim ?? 0) * p.facing;
3433	        if (n21 < -0.75) return 2;
3434	        if (n21 > 0.6) return 4;
3435	        return 3;
3436	      }
3437	      case 14:
3438	      case 15:
3439	      case 10:
3440	      case 16:
3441	        return -1;
3442	      default:
3443	        return pAnim > 2 / 3 ? 1 : pAnim > 1 / 3 ? 2 : 3;
3444	    }
3445	  }

3447	  /** 使用/挥舞动画（与静持同尺寸：原始像素比例，不放大）。
3448	   *  useStyle 1:1 分族（Player.cs:49916 ItemCheck_ApplyUseStyle switch）：
3449	   *   1 挥砍三段（已验证）/ 2 咀嚼 / 3 雨伞 / 4 举过头 / 5 朝鼠标静持（法杖钉中心）
3450	   *   6 饮用旋转 / 9 举杯（DefaultToHealingPotion）/ 13 短剑突刺（复合手臂）/
3451	   *   14-15 头侧持 / 16 委托静持（holdStyle）。
3452	   *  坐标约定：全部按 direction=+1 推导（offX 正=身前），朝左靠镜像空间翻转；
3453	   *  offY 从 p.y（碰撞盒顶）起算；旋转 origin = 底角（PlayerDrawLayers.cs:3260）。
3454	   *  图层：工具/武器挥砍在身体之前调用（身后），使用类托举在身体之后调用（身前） */
3455	  private drawUseItem(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, p: Player, swing: { t: number; dur: number; item: number; useStyle?: number; aim?: number }) {
3456	    const ar = this.atlasIcon(swing.item);
3457	    const icon = ar ? null : this.itemIcon(swing.item);
3458	    const pAnim = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, swing.t / swing.dur));
3459	    const fw = ar ? ar.sw : 14, fh = ar ? ar.sh : 14;
3460	    const us = swing.useStyle ?? 1;
3461	    // → { rot, offX, offY }（dir=1 空间）；hide = 原版把 itemLocation 挪到 -1000（不画）
3462	    let rot = 0, offX = 0, offY = 0, hide = false;
3463	    switch (us) {
3464	      case 1: {
3465	        // useStyle=1（:49939-50108）1:1：约 200° 线性挥砍弧 + 三段持位
3466	        rot = (pAnim - 0.5) * -3.5 - 0.3;
3467	        const xTier = (w: number, phase: 0 | 1 | 2) => phase === 0
3468	          ? w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w > 32 ? 14 : 10
3469	          : phase === 1
3470	            ? w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w > 32 ? 18 : 10
3471	            : w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w >= 48 ? 18 : w > 32 ? 14 : 6;
3472	        const yTier = (h: number, phase: 0 | 1 | 2) => phase === 0 ? 24
3473	          : h > 64 ? 14 : h > 52 ? 12 : phase === 1 && h > 32 ? 8 : 10;
3474	        if (pAnim > 2 / 3) { offX = fw * 0.5 - xTier(fw, 0); offY = yTier(fh, 0); }
3475	        else if (pAnim > 1 / 3) { offX = fw * 0.5 - xTier(fw, 1); offY = yTier(fh, 1); }
3476	        else { offX = -(fw * 0.5 - xTier(fw, 2)); offY = yTier(fh, 2); }
3477	        break;
3478	      }
3479	      case 2: {
3480	        // 咀嚼（:50183-50224，DefaultToFood 非 gulp 族：蘑菇 5/蜂蜜鳍 2314）：
3481	        // 手臂伸缩档 num15=8/6/4/2 → offX = num15+10-fw/2，Y = 中心 + fh/2-4；rot 不变
3482	        const n14 = 1 - pAnim;
3483	        const n15 = n14 <= 0.25 ? 8 : n14 <= 0.5 ? 6 : n14 <= 0.75 ? 4 : 2;
3484	        offX = n15 + 10 - fw * 0.5;
3485	        offY = p.h * 0.5 + fh * 0.5 - 4;
3486	        break;
3487	      }
3488	      case 3: {
3489	        // 雨伞（:50279-50312）：收势段（pAnim>0.666）itemLocation=-1000 不画
3490	        if (pAnim > 0.666) { hide = true; break; }
3491	        rot = 0.8;
3492	        offX = fw * 0.5 - 4 - Math.max(pAnim * fw * 1.2 - 10, 8);
3493	        offY = 24;
3494	        break;

So vi_ keys are `vi_<id>_<name>` where name may contain metal words anywhere (e.g. `vi_3509_copper_pickaxe` → metal word is the 3rd segment; `vi_654_ebonwood_hammer` → no metal). The current `key.split('_')[0]` = 'vi' → metals['vi'] undefined → MATS.wood. For `vi_3509_copper_pickaxe` the correct color would be copper.

Fix: strip the `vi_<id>_` prefix first, then look for a known metal segment anywhere in the remaining name. Implementation:

```
if (def.tool && ['pick', 'axe', 'sword', 'hammer'].includes(def.tool.type)) {
  // vi_<id>_ 前缀剥离后再按首段/任意段找金属色（vi_3509_copper_pickaxe → copper）
  const name = key.replace(/^vi_\d+_/, '');
  const tier = name.split('_').find((seg) => metals[seg]) ?? name.split('_')[0];
  const metal = metals[tier] ?? MATS.wood;
  return drawTool(def.tool.type as 'pick' | 'axe' | 'sword' | 'hammer', metal);
}
```

That satisfies "金属色回退修正" for vi_* keys. For legacy `copper_pick` → tier='copper' ✓ unchanged.

Note drawTool signature accepts 'hammer' in the cast already. Good — that also hints the icon generator supports hammer drawing. Let me verify drawTool handles 'hammer'.
````

50	 *  sheetHeightPx = 素材总像素高（rows×18）；pitch 54 特例组外默认 38 */
51	export function animFrameIdx(sheet: number, sheetHeightPx: number): number {
52	  const rate = TILE_ANIM_RATE[sheet];
53	  if (!rate) return 0;
54	  const pitch = PITCH_54.has(sheet) ? 54 : 38;
55	  const frames = Math.floor(sheetHeightPx / pitch);
56	  if (frames <= 1) return 0;
57	  return Math.floor(tileAnim.tick / rate) % frames;
58	}
59	
60	/** 绘制时的 frameY 偏移（= 帧索引 × pitch） */
61	export function animYOffset(sheet: number, sheetHeightPx: number): number {
62	  const rate = TILE_ANIM_RATE[sheet];
63	  if (!rate) return 0;
64	  const pitch = PITCH_54.has(sheet) ? 54 : 38;
65	  return animFrameIdx(sheet, sheetHeightPx) * pitch;
66	}

```
The rate semantics: `tileFrameCounter[X]++; if (counter > N) { counter=0; frame++; ... }`. Counter increments each tick from 0; when counter > N, advance and reset. So the frame advances every N+1 ticks. Our table: "rate 归一化：原版 counter>N → 每 N+1 tick；counter>=N → 每 N tick". So `>5` → rate 6 ✓ (17: 6 in table ✓).

And `if (++tileFrameCounter[133] >= 4)` → pre-increment then >= 4 → advances every 4 ticks → rate 4.

Let me dump the whole AnimateTiles to extract all the ids and thresholds. It's lines 18366-19155 (~800 lines). Let me grep the pattern compactly.

213	          }
214	        }

227	        const type = st.type[i];
228	        // 原版语义:非活性格不渲染(TileRunner 会给空气格写幽灵 type)
229	        if (type === 0 || !st.flags[i]) continue;
230	        // 已致动(inActive):幽灵态淡显(原版 DrawInactiveSorter,Main.cs:2828 附近)

233	        ctx.globalAlpha = actuated ? 0.3 : 1;

86	
87	  /** 绘制一格墙（源 32×32，目标以格为中心的 2×2 格大小） */
88	  draw(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, wallId: number, truncates: number[], px: number, py: number): void {
89	    const img = this.wallImg(wallId);
90	    if (!img) return;
91	    // 掩码：N=1 W=2 E=4 S=8
92	    let mask = 0;
93	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x, y - 1, truncates)) mask |= 1;
94	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x - 1, y, truncates)) mask |= 2;
95	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x + 1, y, truncates)) mask |= 4;
96	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x, y + 1, truncates)) mask |= 8;
97	    // 全邻 → 中心子模式
98	    if (mask === 15) mask += CENTER[x % 3][y % 3];
99	    // 变体（largeFrame 三模式）
100	    const lf = this.atlas ? this.atlas.vanilla.walls[String(wallId)]?.largeFrame ?? 0 : 0;
101	    let v: number;
102	    if (lf === 1) v = PHLEBAS[y % 4][x % 3] - 1;
103	    else if (lf === 2) v = LAZURE[x % 2][y % 2] - 1;
104	    else v = ((x * 7) + (y * 11)) % 3;
105	    const entry = WALL_LOOKUP[mask];
106	    if (!entry) return;
107	    const fr = entry[v] ?? entry[0];
108	    const sx = fr[0] * STRIDE, sy = fr[1] * STRIDE;
109	    if (sx + 32 > img.width || sy + 32 > img.height) return; // 变体3 超出矮表（默认模式不会发生）
110	    ctx.drawImage(img, sx, sy, 32, 32, px - TILE / 2, py - TILE / 2, 32, 32);
111	  }
112	
113	  /** 墙帧（col,row）——调试/纠错面板定位用 */
114	  frameAt(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, wallId: number, truncates: number[]): { col: number; row: number } {
115	    let mask = 0;
116	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x, y - 1, truncates)) mask |= 1;
117	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x - 1, y, truncates)) mask |= 2;
118	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x + 1, y, truncates)) mask |= 4;
119	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x, y + 1, truncates)) mask |= 8;
120	    if (mask === 15) mask += CENTER[x % 3][y % 3];
121	    const lf = this.atlas ? this.atlas.vanilla.walls[String(wallId)]?.largeFrame ?? 0 : 0;
122	    let v: number;
123	    if (lf === 1) v = PHLEBAS[y % 4][x % 3] - 1;
124	    else if (lf === 2) v = LAZURE[x % 2][y % 2] - 1;
125	    else v = ((x * 7) + (y * 11)) % 3;
126	    const fr = WALL_LOOKUP[mask]?.[v] ?? [1, 1];
127	    return { col: fr[0], row: fr[1] };
128	  }
129	
130	  hasTexture(wallId: number): boolean {
131	    return this.wallImg(wallId) !== null;
132	  }
133	}
134	

```

Let me restructure: define `const seg1 = (ph: 0|1|2)`... Actually I'll define a local helper before the switch:

    // useStyle 1 三段持位（Player.cs:49957-50095 1:1）——case 1 与 default 共用
    const swingTier = (ph: 0 | 1 | 2): { x: number; y: number } => {
      const xT = (w: number) => ph === 0
        ? w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w > 32 ? 14 : 10
        : ph === 1
          ? w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w > 32 ? 18 : 10
          : w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w >= 48 ? 18 : w > 32 ? 14 : 6;
      const yT = (h: number) => ph === 0 ? 24
        : h > 64 ? 14 : h > 52 ? 12 : ph === 1 && h > 32 ? 8 : 10;
      return ph === 2
        ? { x: -(fw * 0.5 - xT(fw)), y: yT(fh) }
        : { x: fw * 0.5 - xT(fw), y: yT(fh) };
    };
```

 "new_string": "const STRIDE = 36; // 32px 帧 + 4px 间隔\n\n// ============ 墙动画（Main.DoUpdate_AnimateWalls，Main.cs:19181-19305）============\n// 动画墙表 468×1440 = 13 列 × 40 行（36px/行）＝ 8 个动画带 × 5 行/带\n// （WallDrawing.cs:119 `wallFrameY + wallFrame[wall]*180`，180px=5 行）。\n// rate 归一化同 tile 表：counter>=N → 每 N tick\nexport const WALL_ANIM_RATE: Record<number, number> = {\n  136: 5, 137: 10, 168: 5, 169: 5, 172: 10, 225: 10, 226: 10, 227: 5,\n  347: 10, 357: 5, 365: 8,\n};\n\n/** 242/243 星彩玻璃（WallDrawing.cs:122-127）：不走全局 wallFrame，逐格错相——\n *  (counter + x*11 + y*27) % 160，每 20 tick 一带共 8 带（counter 每 tick +1） */\nexport function starGlassBand(tick: number, x: number, y: number): number {\n  return Math.floor((((tick + x * 11 + y * 27) % 160) + 160) % 160 / 20);\n}\n\n/** 该墙当前动画带（无动画返回 0）。225 回卷 2 带（Main.cs:19241-19249 `>1`），\n *  其余 8 带（`>7`） */\nexport function wallAnimFrame(wallId: number): number {\n  const rate = WALL_ANIM_RATE[wallId];\n  if (!rate) return 0;\n  const frames = wallId === 225 ? 2 : 8;\n  return Math.floor(tileAnim.tick / rate) % frames;\n}\n\n/** 换帧行列的推进节奏（ChunkCache.advanceAnim 重烘焙判定用）：\n *  0=无动画；1=逐格错相（星彩玻璃，每 tick 都有格换带）；否则=表内 rate */\nexport function wallAnimRate(wallId: number): number {\n  if (wallId === 242 || wallId === 243) return 1;\n  return WALL_ANIM_RATE[wallId] ?? 0;\n}\n\n/** 动画带 → 本渲染器行偏移（5 行/带） */\nexport function wallAnimBandRows(wallId: number, x: number, y: number): number {\n  if (wallId === 242 || wallId === 243) return starGlassBand(tileAnim.tick, x, y) * 5;\n  return wallAnimFrame(wallId) * 5;\n}"

        wallFrame[num] = 0;
    }
}
```
→ rate 5. I wrote 10 — wrong. Fix to 5.

Also double check each:
- 357: >=5 → 5 ✓
- 365: >=8 → 8 ✓
- 136: >=5 → 5 ✓
- 137: >=10 → 10 ✓
- 226: >=10 → 10 ✓
- 227: >=5 → 5 ✓
- 172: >=10 → 10 ✓
- 347: >=10 → 10 ✓
- 168: >=5 → 5 ✓
- 169: >=5 → 5 ✓
- 225: >=5 → 5 (fix)
````
